So, no, I wouldn't say I feel hopeless about it.\n\nDoctor: Are you enjoying activities or hobbies that you normally like to do?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I used to get pretty excited about going to gallery nights, or playing basketball, even just grabbing food-truck tacos or helping kids with art stuff. But lately... I don't know. If I do go out, I just stand there feeling flat. I usually just end up making an excuse so I can leave early.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way about the things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: It's been about two months. Yeah, right around two months. It started right after I got this window display award at the store, and the district manager started giving me all this attention. That's when I stopped really wanting to do much else.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're empty or numb inside most of the time?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say empty or numb. I mean, I still feel things... I just don't really have the energy or the interest for the stuff I used to do. But I don't feel completely blank inside.\n\nDoctor: Would you say you've lost a significant amount of weight, like more than 5% of your body weight, in the past two months?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I probably have. Honestly, that's kind of what made me finally ask for help. I just... I barely want to eat anymore. Like, I'll be on my lunch break at the store, just staring at food I used to really like, and I just end up throwing most of it away.\n\nDoctor: Are you afraid of gaining weight?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. It's not about the weight. I just... I don't have any appetite. The thought of eating just doesn't appeal to me right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt a period of time where you felt sad or depressed for most of the day?\n\nPatient: I mean, yeah, definitely. That's... that's kind of the main thing. I just feel really down and sad most of the day, almost every day lately. It just feels like this heavy cloud I can't shake off.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I'll just sit there on my break, looking at a sandwich or whatever, and I just can't bring myself to eat it. So I end up throwing it out. I'm pretty sure I've dropped some weight because of it.\n\nDoctor: How much weight do you think you've lost?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, honestly. I don't own a scale, so I haven't weighed myself or anything. I just know my clothes are fitting a bit looser than they used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling hopeless about the future lately?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean... I don't really think about the future all that much, honestly. I just go to work at the store and get through my shifts.
